Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the electrochemistry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 544. The moduli space has dimension at most 165 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the electrochemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^544 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 165-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on electrochemistry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 832. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 743 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 832 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electrochemistry structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 743. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
